DIMENSIONS:     1950 × 884 px — recommended **double** column — ~284 DPI at 6.875 in (~273 DPI at the 181 mm IEEE double-column measure); 600 DPI if forced to 3.25 in single column
RESOLUTION:     **Below threshold** — line art (vector plots, bar charts, curve overlays); 284 DPI at double-column width vs. 300 DPI minimum / 600 DPI preferred
FORMAT:         **Needs conversion** — PNG → TIFF (LZW) or, preferably, native vector PDF/EPS
FILE SIZE:      OK — 0.17 MB (limit 10 MB)
TEXT RENDERING: **Rasterized** — PNG raster; letterforms and thin axis spines will show pixel edges at print scale
COLOR MODE:     OK (assumed RGB) — journal requires RGB; note the saturated default-palette blue (#1f77b4) and orange (#ff7f0e) are the two most at risk if any downstream CMYK conversion occurs

ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] Resolution insufficient: at the required double-column width the effective resolution is ~273–284 DPI, below the 300 DPI floor and far below the 600 DPI preference for line art. A 4×2 panel grid of thin-line plots is the worst case for under-sampling — hairline spines, tick marks and the sinc ripples in (c) will alias.
  - [BLOCKING] Rasterized text in a line-art figure. All labels, the LaTeX-style math in the suptitle and the ν values are pixel data, not glyphs.
  - [BLOCKING] Text too small at final size. Tick labels measure roughly 16–18 px, i.e. ≈4–4.5 pt when the figure is placed at 181 mm width — below the 6 pt absolute minimum and well below the 8–10 pt target. The multi-line panel headers are similarly ≈4.5–5 pt.
  - [BLOCKING] Figure contains an internal title. The overall suptitle ("Every estimator is a bank of tapers with weights, Ŝ(f) = Σ_k c_k|v_kᵀx_f|² : three ways to build the bank from one window (N = 256, W = 4/N)") is a title inside the figure and is not permitted; it must move into the caption.
  - [ADVISORY] The three-line descriptive text above each top-row panel functions as a per-panel title/annotation. IEEE tolerates short axis-level annotations, but this much prose belongs in the caption; keeping it also forces the tiny type size flagged above.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel labelling is incomplete and non-standard. Labels (a)–(d) exist only in the top row, embedded in the title text; the four bottom panels are unlabelled. The caption refers to "Top:" and "Bottom:", but IEEE style expects discrete lowercase labels, i.e. (a)–(d) top and (e)–(h) bottom, each set flush at the panel corner.
  - [ADVISORY] Statistical reporting is only half satisfied. The estimator degrees of freedom ν are stated numerically in each bottom panel (13.4, 11.1, 17.2, 14.0) and the caption points to Eq. (ladder) — good. However, the figure/caption never states how many windows/segments/realizations underlie each bank (7 Hann segments in (a), N = 256 taper offsets in (c) with only 4 drawn, K = 7 in (d)), nor whether the curves are analytic (deterministic tapers) or averages over realizations. Since ν is being compared across four estimators, state explicitly that these are deterministic taper banks with no Monte-Carlo averaging, and give the number of tapers summed for each ν.
  - [ADVISORY] Caption states "(4 shown)" for (c) only in the panel header, not in the caption text; a reader of the caption alone will not know that the (c) bank is truncated for display.
  - [ADVISORY] The seven-curve palette in (a) includes brown and pink, which are difficult to separate from red and magenta in grayscale or for red-green colour-vision deficiency. Curves are vertically offset in (b)–(d) but not in (a), where they overlap.
  - [ADVISORY] Top-row panels have no y-axis label or units; amplitude is arbitrary/offset. State "taper amplitude (offset for display, arbitrary units)".

SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Re-export from the plotting source as vector PDF or EPS at exactly 181 mm (7.13 in) width. This resolves the resolution, format and rasterized-text issues in one step. If a raster deliverable is mandatory, render TIFF at ≥600 DPI for 181 mm, i.e. ≥4280 px wide (≥1940 px tall), LZW-compressed.
  - Set font sizes in the source so that, at 181 mm final width, tick labels are ≥7 pt, axis labels 8 pt and any in-panel annotation ≥7 pt. In matplotlib: create the figure with `figsize=(7.13, 3.23)` and set `rcParams['font.size']=8`, `font.family='sans-serif'`, `font.sans-serif=['Arial','Helvetica']`, then `savefig(..., format='pdf')` — do not scale a large figure down, which is what has produced the 4 pt text here.
  - Delete the suptitle from the figure and fold its content into the first sentence of the caption, e.g. "Every estimator is a bank of tapers with weights, Ŝ(f)=Σ_k c_k|v_kᵀ x_f|²; three ways to build the bank from one window (N = 256, W = 4/N)."
  - Replace the three-line panel headers with bare lowercase labels (a)–(h) placed at the upper-left of each axes, and move the descriptive text into the caption, keyed by label. Suggested short in-panel tags if any are kept: "(a) Welch, Hann, L = N/4", "(b) Hann + Gaussian, σ = 3/N", "(c) sinc, W = 4/N, all offsets", "(d) Thomson, K = 7".
  - Add the sample counts and DOF provenance to the caption: "Banks are deterministic (no realization averaging); ν is computed from the plotted weights by Eq. (ladder) over 7, 16, 256 and 7 tapers for (a)–(d) respectively, with only the first four tapers of (c) drawn."
  - Add a y-axis label to the top row and note the vertical offset; add "(offset for clarity)" to panels (b)–(d).
  - For (a), either offset the seven Hann segments vertically as in the other panels or distinguish them by line style as well as colour so the figure survives grayscale reproduction.
  - Keep the file in RGB; do not pre-convert to CMYK. If the production house requests CMYK, substitute a darker blue and a deeper orange (or switch to a print-safe qualitative palette such as Okabe–Ito) to avoid visible hue shift.

The figure is a single EEG epoch (one realization) — state that explicitly so readers do not read the spread as an across-subject/across-epoch error bar.

  - [ADVISORY] Panel (g) shows a duplicated y-axis label ("dB" printed twice, apparently colliding with the colorbar label of (d)); panel (h) y-axis reads "pixels" while the caption says "all pixels" — reconcile.
  - [ADVISORY] Panels (c) and (e) have no colorbar; the single colorbar sits beside (d). If (c), (d), (e) share one dB scale this must be stated explicitly, otherwise readers cannot compare them.
  - [ADVISORY] Statistical reporting incomplete. The caption gives ν for each estimator (good, and it satisfies the degrees-of-freedom requirement for the noise comparison), but the number of realizations behind the distributions/summary statistics is not stated: (h) needs the total pixel count N (and the number of 2-s windows, ~589 at 1-s step, × number of frequency bins) that generate the histogram and the "median |diff| 1.5 dB" figure. Panels (f,g) are single-window spectra — state that explicitly (no averaging, one realization) so the ν values are not misread as window counts.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel (a) is clipped at ±500 µV; the clipping is in the caption but not annotated on the axis — a note or clipped-sample marker would prevent misreading the ictal amplitude.
  - [ADVISORY] Jet colormap: not colorblind-safe and creates false banding at the ~40 dB yellow band, which is exactly where the ictal contrast lives.
SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Re-export from the plotting source at 181 mm width with ≥300 DPI for the embedded spectrogram arrays and vector line/text for everything else: e.g. `fig.set_size_inches(7.16, 6.13)` then save as PDF (`savefig('figure8.pdf')`) with `rcParams['pdf.fonttype']=42`; supply an accompanying 600 DPI TIFF if the publisher requires raster.
  - If a pure raster deliverable is mandated, render at ≥2140 x 1832 px (300 DPI at 181 mm); 4290 x 3670 px gives 600 DPI and will still be well under 10 MB as LZW-compressed TIFF.
  - Set base font size to 8 pt at final size (labels/ticks 8 pt, legend 7–8 pt) after fixing the physical width; verify no label falls below 6 pt.
  - Delete all in-panel titles; keep only bold lowercase "(a)"–"(h)" in a panel corner and migrate the descriptive strings (window length, NW, K, ν, W) into the caption, where most already appear.
  - Fix the overlapping/duplicated "dB" labels between panel (d)'s colorbar and panel (g)'s y-axis; add `constrained_layout` or explicit axes padding.
  - Add a shared colorbar spanning (c)–(e) (or one per panel) and state in the caption that the three spectrograms use an identical dB scale and identical window/step parameters.
  - Replace jet with a perceptually uniform, print-safe map (viridis, or turbo if a rainbow is required) to remove banding and reduce CMYK-shift risk.
  - Extend the caption with the realization counts, e.g.: "(c–e) 589 windows of 2 s stepped by 1 s; (h) histogram of N = 589 x 61 = 35 929 time–frequency pixels; median |difference| 1.5 dB. (f,g) single-window estimates (one realization each); ν as listed quantifies the smoothing, not a window count."
  - Confirm the PNG is true RGB (not palette-indexed) before conversion, and embed an sRGB profile in the delivered TIFF/PDF.

### figure9.png

---
DIMENSIONS:     1376 x 978 px — recommended **double column** — ~200 DPI at 6.875 in (192 DPI at the full 181 mm / 7.16 in trim width; 423 DPI if forced to 3.25 in single column)
RESOLUTION:     Below threshold — mixed figure (continuous-tone spectrogram rasters in b–d + line-art trace/axes in a): 200 DPI at the recommended double-column width, vs. 300 DPI minimum (600 preferred for the line-art/text layer). Aspect ratio (1.41:1) is well suited to double column: at 6.875 in wide the height is ~4.9 in (124 mm), comfortably under the 230 mm limit.
FORMAT:         Needs conversion for final submission — PNG → TIFF (LZW/ZIP) or, preferably, a hybrid PDF/EPS with vector axes/text and embedded raster image panels. PNG is acceptable for review only.
FILE SIZE:      OK — 1.48 MB (limit 10 MB); ample headroom to re-export at 600 DPI.
TEXT RENDERING: Rasterized — all axis labels, tick numbers, panel titles and the colorbar label are pixel-based (PNG export); no vector text layer.
COLOR MODE:     OK (RGB assumed for PNG) — but the "jet" colormap is the main risk: its saturated red, cyan and blue extremes are exactly the hues that shift most under any RGB→CMYK conversion by the publisher, and jet is perceptually non-uniform and poorly separable for red–green colorblind readers. Panel (a)'s pale pink seizure shading is very low-contrast and may wash out in print or in grayscale reproduction.
ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] Effective resolution of ~200 DPI at double-column width is below the 300 DPI minimum for the halftone spectrogram panels and far below the 600 DPI preference for the line-art/text content.
  - [BLOCKING] Rasterized text in a line-art/mixed figure. Additionally, if the 1376 px canvas is placed at 6.875 in the text is scaled ~0.5×: matplotlib-default 10 pt labels become ~5 pt, below the 6 pt absolute floor and outside the 8–10 pt target. Tick numbers in (a) and the colorbar label are the smallest and worst affected.
  - [ADVISORY] Panels carry in-figure titles ("(a) scalp EEG, channel FP1 (common-average reference); 2 seizures shaded", "(b) Hann periodogram, 2-s windows: ν = 2", etc.). The journal prohibits titles inside the figure; only lowercase panel labels (a)–(d) are permitted.
  - [ADVISORY] Statistical/estimator reporting is incomplete. The equivalent degrees of freedom are given (ν = 2, 6, 4.8) — good — but the number of realizations behind each estimate is not stated: window length is given (2 s) yet the overlap/hop, the total number of windows per spectrogram, the sampling rate, and the number of frequency bins averaged by the 1 Hz box smoother are missing. Because this figure explicitly compares noise levels between estimators, these must appear in the caption or Methods.
  - [ADVISORY] Colorbar is labelled only "dB" with no reference quantity (e.g., dB re 1 µV²/Hz) and no statement of whether all three panels share one color scale (they appear to, but the shared colorbar is placed only beside panels b–c).
  - [ADVISORY] Channel naming inconsistent: axis label and panel title use "FP1", caption uses "Fp1".
  - [ADVISORY] Jet colormap: non-uniform lightness creates false banding/contours in the harmonic stacks and is not colorblind-safe; saturated extremes are CMYK-shift-prone.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel (a) x-axis has tick marks but no numeric labels; readers must map to panel (d)'s shared time axis. Acceptable if intentional, but the shared axis should be stated in the caption.
  - [ADVISORY] Caption says "clipped at ±500 µV" while the y-axis spans exactly ±500 µV — confirm that visible flat-topped excursions are clipping artifacts, and note this in the caption so they are not read as signal.
SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Re-generate from source at the final physical size: set figsize to 7.16 × 5.0 in and save with `dpi=600` (→ ~4300 × 3000 px) for the raster panels; set spectrogram `pcolormesh`/`imshow` with `rasterized=True` and save as PDF or EPS so axes, ticks and labels remain vector while image panels stay raster. Then supply a 600 DPI TIFF only if vector formats are refused.
  - Set fonts explicitly for the final size: Arial/Helvetica, 8 pt tick labels, 9–10 pt axis labels, never below 6 pt after any scaling.
  - Delete all four in-figure titles; place lowercase labels (a), (b), (c), (d) in the upper-left corner of each panel and move the descriptive text ("Hann periodogram, 2-s windows, ν = 2", etc.) into the caption.
  - Replace jet with viridis or turbo (perceptually ordered, print- and colorblind-robust); if the diverging warm/cool look is required, use a lightness-monotonic alternative and verify a grayscale proof.
  - Darken or outline the seizure shading in (a) (e.g., 20–25% gray fill with a thin border, or bracket markers above the trace) so it survives print and grayscale.
  - Extend the caption with the estimator bookkeeping, e.g.: "Sampling rate f_s = ___ Hz. Spectrograms use 2-s windows with ___ s hop (___ % overlap), giving ___ windows across the 600 s record. Hann periodogram: ν = 2 (one taper, one window per estimate). Multitaper: NW = 2, K = 3 tapers, ν = 2K = 6. Box smoothing of half-width 1 Hz averages ___ adjacent frequency bins, giving ν ≈ 4.8. All three panels share the color scale shown, in dB re 1 µV²/Hz."
  - Unify channel naming to "Fp1" in the axis label, panel text and caption.
  - Move or duplicate the colorbar so it is unambiguously shared by (b), (c) and (d) — e.g., a single colorbar spanning the full height of the three spectrogram panels.
  - Confirm final export is RGB and add a note to the production editor requesting no automatic CMYK conversion, or supply a CMYK-safe proof of the chosen colormap.

## Caption & Legend Auditor

### efigure1.png

---
**CAPTION UNDER REVIEW:** The submitted LaTeX block is contaminated — it opens with the caption/body of a *table* ("Band powers from the everyday estimators…", `\label{tab:bandpower}`) plus several pages of appendix text, and the caption that actually belongs to this figure appears only at the very end ("**Kernels at the same design bandwidth**…", `\includegraphics{fig1_kernels.png}`). The review below evaluates that final caption. The filename mismatch (`efigure1.png` vs. `fig1_kernels.png`) must also be resolved.

TITLE:              Present & bold — "**Kernels at the same design bandwidth**" (good; concise and informative). However, an additional *in-figure* title ("Expected-value smoothing kernels at equal design bandwidth (N=256, NW=4)") duplicates it and violates the journal's no-title-inside-figure rule.

PANEL DESCRIPTIONS: Partial — both panels are described ("linear scale near the main lobe (left)" / "in dB (right)"), but the figure carries no lowercase (a)/(b) panel labels, so the caption cannot key to them as the journal requires.

ERROR BARS:         Not present — correct and acceptable; these are deterministic expected-value kernels. The caption should say so explicitly ("deterministic expected-value kernels; no stochastic realizations, hence no error bars") so a reader does not wonder.

SAMPLE SIZE:        Not applicable in the usual sense, but **missing the required substitute**: IEEE TBME asks for the estimator degrees of freedom wherever noise levels are compared. The caption compares leakage/skirts across estimators and defers ν to Table 1 rather than quoting it. Also missing: FFT/zero-padding grid length used to render the kernels, and the box half-width in the same units as the axis.

STATISTICAL DETAIL: Not applicable — no hypothesis test is performed; nothing to report. (Confirm no test is implied by "differ by 40 dB", which is a descriptive, not inferential, claim.)

ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: **NW** (in-figure title; the caption uses W = 4/N instead, so the reader must derive NW = time–bandwidth product); **K** (number of tapers — the in-figure legend gives K = 2NW−1 = 7 but never says K is the taper count); **dB** (acceptable as standard); **W** is given numerically but never named ("design half-bandwidth"). "Hann" and "Bohman" are window names and should be identified as such ("Hann- and Bohman-tapered periodograms").

COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Present in-figure (left panel legend: dotted black = ideal box, red = multitaper, blue = raw periodogram + box, green = Hann + box, orange = Bohman + box). Advisory: the right panel has **no legend** and reuses the same colors; the caption should state that the color code is shared across panels, and that the vertical dotted lines in the right panel mark ±W.

SELF-CONTAINED:     No — the caption points to `Table~\ref{tab:dof}` for the quantitative backbone (bandwidth, degrees of freedom, leakage), the units of the frequency axis ("frequency (1/N)" = cycles/sample × N, i.e., normalized bins) are never explained, the left-panel y-axis scaling ("kernel (linear, × N)") is never explained, and the right-panel dB reference (peak = 0 dB?) is not stated.

ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] Caption/figure mismatch: the supplied LaTeX contains a table caption (`tab:bandpower`, NW = 2) and appendix prose ahead of the figure caption, and the `\includegraphics` filename (`fig1_kernels.png`) differs from the submitted file (`efigure1.png`). As delivered, the reviewer/editor cannot bind caption to figure. Resolve before resubmission.
  - [BLOCKING] Degrees of freedom not stated in the legend. The figure's whole argument is a leakage-vs-variance trade at matched bandwidth; ν for each of the four estimators must appear in the caption itself, not only by cross-reference to a table.
  - [BLOCKING] Axis quantities undefined in the legend: "frequency (1/N)" and "kernel (linear, × N)" are idiosyncratic normalizations that no reader can reconstruct from the caption alone. Also state the dB normalization reference for the right panel.
  - [BLOCKING] No panel labels (a)/(b) in the figure; journal style requires lowercase panel labels keyed in the caption.
  - [ADVISORY] In-figure suptitle and the two per-axes titles ("main lobe", "sidelobes = leakage") violate the "no titles inside the figure" rule; move this content into the caption and delete from the image.
  - [ADVISORY] Right panel lacks a color legend and the two vertical dotted lines are unexplained.
  - [ADVISORY] "the skirts differ by 40 dB" is imprecise — specify at what frequency offset and between which two curves (blue vs. orange appears to be ~75 dB at |f| = 20/N, not 40).
  - [ADVISORY] K = 2NW − 1 = 7 is stated only in the in-figure legend; if the suptitle is removed, NW = 4 disappears and the relation becomes unreadable.
  - [ADVISORY] Figure is 1430 px wide; at 181 mm double-column this is ~200 DPI, below the 300 DPI minimum (600 DPI preferred for line art). Re-export as vector (PDF/EPS) or ≥2140 px.
  - [ADVISORY] Tick/axis label sizes should be checked against the 8–10 pt minimum after scaling to 181 mm; the current in-figure legend text will fall near 6 pt.

SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Delete the in-figure suptitle and both axes titles; add lowercase (a) and (b) at the upper-left of each panel; add the color legend (or a note "colors as in (a)") to panel (b).
  - Re-export as PDF/EPS vector at 181 mm width, RGB, with all text ≥8 pt at final size.
  - Replace the caption with a self-contained version, e.g.:

    "**Expected-value smoothing kernels of four estimators at matched design bandwidth.** Kernels H(f) for N = 256 samples and design half-bandwidth W = 4/N (time–bandwidth product NW = 4, i.e. full bandwidth 2W = 8/N). Frequency is in units of 1/N (normalized frequency × N, i.e. DFT bins). (a) Linear scale near the main lobe; the ordinate is the kernel multiplied by N so that a unit-area kernel of half-width W has height 1/(2NW) = 0.125. (b) Same kernels in dB, normalized to the kernel peak, over a wider frequency range; vertical dotted lines mark ±W. Curves: ideal box of half-width W (black dotted); multitaper with all K = 2NW − 1 = 7 Slepian tapers, equal weights (red); untapered (raw) periodogram convolved with a box of half-width W (blue); Hann-tapered periodogram then box (green); Bohman-tapered periodogram then box (orange). Colors are identical in (a) and (b). All four main lobes have half-power full widths within X % of one another (see Table I), but the sidelobe skirts differ by up to ~Y dB at |f| = 20/N. Equivalent degrees of freedom at an interior frequency are ν = … (multitaper), … (raw + box), … (Hann + box), … (Bohman + box). Kernels are deterministic expected-value quantities computed in closed form on a Z-point zero-padded grid; no Monte Carlo realizations are involved, hence no error bars. dB, decibels."

  - Fill in X, Y, Z and the four ν values numerically so the caption no longer depends on Table I.
  - Confirm the caption is placed below the figure and that the file/label names match (`efigure1.png` ↔ `\label{fig:kernels}`), and remove the stray table caption and appendix text from the figure-caption submission.

### efigure2.png

---
TITLE:              Present & bold ("**Dropping the leakiest taper.**")
PANEL DESCRIPTIONS: Not applicable (single panel)
ERROR BARS:         Not present — but noise-floor/variance claims are made without stating the estimator degrees of freedom or number of realizations (see ISSUES)
SAMPLE SIZE:        Missing — "Another realization" implies n = 1 realization, but this is never stated explicitly; no window/segment count given for the box-smoothed estimates
STATISTICAL DETAIL: Partial — no hypothesis test is used (acceptable), but the journal's noise-comparison requirement is unmet: missing (i) equivalent degrees of freedom for each estimator (2K = 14 for K = 7, 12 for K = 6; effective dof of the box smoothers), (ii) box-smoother span in bins/Hz, (iii) time–bandwidth product (NW = 4) and how the K = 7 vs K = 6 choice relates to 2NW − 1, (iv) how "floors 20 dB high"/"10 dB high" were quantified (frequency band used, mean or median bias in dB)
ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: PSD (power spectral density); AR (autoregressive) is implied but not expanded; dB axis units not tied to a reference (dB relative to what? variance per cycle/sample); K (number of tapers) and W (half-bandwidth) not defined in words; "AR4" in the in-figure legend is inconsistent with "AR(4)" in the caption
COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Present (in-figure legend), but caption order does not match legend order and line colors are not named in the caption
SELF-CONTAINED:     No — reader cannot reproduce or interpret the comparison without the AR(4) coefficients, the smoothing span, the taper parameters/dof, which taper was dropped, and whether one or many realizations are shown
ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] Estimator degrees of freedom are not stated anywhere although the entire figure compares noise floors and variances. IEEE TBME requires dof when noise levels are compared.
  - [BLOCKING] Number of realizations/windows behind each trace is not stated. "Another realization" is ambiguous; if n = 1 this must be declared, and the reader must be told that the visible roughness is single-realization variability, not an error band.
  - [BLOCKING] The box-smoothing bandwidth (number of frequency bins or Hz, and whether it matches 2W) is never given, so "box-smoothed" is undefined and the comparison with the K-taper estimates is uninterpretable.
  - [BLOCKING] In-figure title "AR(4) process, N=1024, design bandwidth W=4/N" violates the journal's no-title-inside-figure rule; this information belongs in the caption only.
  - [BLOCKING] Image resolution is insufficient: 1040 px across a 181 mm double-column figure ≈ 146 DPI, below the 300 DPI minimum (600 DPI preferred for line art). Supply vector EPS/PDF or a ≥300 DPI raster.
  - [ADVISORY] "Drop the leakiest taper" does not identify which taper (the highest-order DPSS, k = 6, zero-indexed) nor the criterion for "leakiest" (e.g., smallest eigenvalue / largest out-of-band fraction).
  - [ADVISORY] Caption enumerates the five curves in a different order from the in-figure legend and does not name colors, making cross-matching slow. The Hann trace is listed last in the caption but fourth in the legend.
  - [ADVISORY] Quantitative claims ("floors 20 dB high", "floors 10 dB high") are given for two curves only; the bias of the K = 6 and Hann traces is not quantified, so the figure's main message ("dropping the leakiest taper helps") is asserted rather than measured.
  - [ADVISORY] AR(4) parameters (pole locations/coefficients) and the sampling convention are not given, so the true PSD cannot be reproduced.
  - [ADVISORY] dB ordinate lacks a stated reference level; axis label should read e.g. "power (dB re unit variance per cycle/sample)".
  - [ADVISORY] Grey periodogram trace largely obscures the dashed true PSD in the 0.25–0.5 band; consider plotting the true PSD on top or thinning the periodogram alpha (figure-craft note affecting caption claims about the floor).
SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Rewrite as: "**Dropping the leakiest taper reduces the broadband noise floor.** Spectrum estimates for a single realization (n = 1) of a stationary AR(4) process (coefficients in Table I; N = 1024 samples, design half-bandwidth W = 4/N, i.e. NW = 4). Grey: raw periodogram (2 dof). Blue: untapered periodogram smoothed with a 9-bin box (≈18 dof), whose noise floor sits ≈20 dB above the true PSD above 0.3 cycles/sample. Green: Hann-tapered periodogram with the same 9-bin box smoother. Red: equal-weight multitaper estimate with K = 7 DPSS tapers (2K = 14 dof), floor ≈10 dB high. Orange: same with the leakiest (highest-order, k = 6) taper dropped, K = 6 (2K = 12 dof). Dashed black: true AR(4) PSD. Bias values are median differences from the true PSD over 0.30–0.50 cycles/sample. No error bars are shown; visible roughness is single-realization variability. PSD, power spectral density; AR, autoregressive; DPSS, discrete prolate spheroidal sequence."
  - Delete the in-figure title and move "AR(4) process, N = 1024, W = 4/N" into the caption.
  - Reorder the caption's curve list to match the in-figure legend order and name each color explicitly.
  - Add the box-smoother span and the equivalent dof of every estimator; if the smoothers were tuned to equal resolution 2W, say so.
  - State the criterion used to label taper k = 6 "leakiest" (eigenvalue λ₆, or out-of-band energy fraction).
  - Re-export as vector PDF/EPS (or ≥600 DPI TIFF) sized to 181 mm width with 8–10 pt sans-serif labels; verify the legend text is ≥6 pt at final size.
  - Optionally add a second panel showing bias vs. K (or vs. frequency) so the "10 dB → floor at true PSD" improvement is quantified rather than eyeballed; if added, label panels (a) and (b) lowercase and describe both in order.

### efigure3.png

---
TITLE:              Present & bold — but duplicated by an in-figure suptitle that must be deleted (see ISSUES)
PANEL DESCRIPTIONS: Complete in content, non-compliant in form — panels are addressed as "Left/Center/Right" instead of lowercase (a), (b), (c), and no panel labels exist in the image
ERROR BARS:         Not present — and not addressed. Because these are deterministic kernel quantities, the legend must say so explicitly; the journal also requires the estimator's degrees of freedom to be stated wherever noise/variance levels are compared, and ν is plotted but never defined
SAMPLE SIZE:        Missing — no statement of whether curves are analytic/deterministic or Monte-Carlo averages; if deterministic, state "no realizations; quantities computed in closed form from each smoothing kernel"
STATISTICAL DETAIL: Not applicable in the hypothesis-testing sense, but INCOMPLETE as written — missing: (i) definition of ν (equivalent degrees of freedom) and its relation to K ("2K-like"); (ii) the sweep parameter and its range for each method (NW range, box width range, Gaussian σ range, Welch segment length / segment count); (iii) statement that no averaging over realizations is involved
ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: ν; dof; DPSS; NW; K; dB (acceptable but define once if space); "box" (boxcar/rectangular frequency-domain smoother); N (record length in samples); "1/N" as the bandwidth unit (Rayleigh bin)
COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Present in panel (a) only — legend must state that the key in (a) applies to all three panels
SELF-CONTAINED:     No — reader cannot interpret the left ordinate (ν / "equivalent dof (2K-like)"), does not know how many sine tapers were used, does not know the Welch segment configuration, does not know the axis units, and does not know the plots are noiseless/deterministic
ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] In-figure suptitle "Resolution / variance / leakage per method, N=256 (the 2015 whiteboard plan)" violates the journal's no-title-inside-figure rule; the parenthetical is an internal working note and must not appear in a submitted figure. Delete the suptitle entirely (the three per-panel headings are also titles — convert them to (a)/(b)/(c) labels with the descriptive text moved into the caption, or keep them as short axis-area annotations only if the editor permits).
  - [BLOCKING] Error-bar/variability statement absent. Even for deterministic curves the legend must state that no error bars are shown and why ("each curve is computed analytically from the estimator's smoothing kernel; no Monte-Carlo realizations, hence no error bars").
  - [BLOCKING] ν is used in the caption and "equivalent dof (2K-like)" on the axis without definition. Since this panel is precisely a noise-level/variance comparison, the estimator degrees of freedom must be defined explicitly per the journal's statistical-reporting requirement.
  - [BLOCKING] Panel identification does not follow journal style: no (a), (b), (c) labels in the image and caption uses Left/Center/Right.
  - [BLOCKING] Resolution: 1820 px across a 181 mm double-column width is ≈255 DPI, below the 300 DPI minimum (600 DPI preferred for line art). Re-export as vector PDF/EPS or ≥600 DPI TIFF.
  - [ADVISORY] Sweep parameters and their ranges are not given for any method, so the abscissa cannot be reproduced. State, e.g., NW = 1.5–8 for the multitaper curves, boxcar widths of m bins, Gaussian σ range, Welch segment lengths L with 50% overlap.
  - [ADVISORY] Number of sine tapers is unspecified (K = ? for the sine-taper curve).
  - [ADVISORY] Axis units: "half-power bandwidth (1/N)" should be spelled out as "full half-power bandwidth in units of the Rayleigh resolution 1/N (cycles/sample)".
  - [ADVISORY] Logarithmic ordinate in panel (b) is not mentioned; state "note logarithmic ordinate spanning ~7 decades".
  - [ADVISORY] Curves terminate at different maximum bandwidths (e.g., Hann→Gaussian stops near 10/N, Welch reaches 25/N) with no explanation; state the constraint that truncates each sweep.
  - [ADVISORY] Colors alone distinguish eight methods; red/brown and blue/purple pairs are hard to separate at 88–181 mm and in grayscale. Add distinct markers per method and mention them in the key.
  - [ADVISORY] Legend box occupies a large fraction of panel (a) and its type looks close to the 6 pt floor at final size; verify ≥8 pt at printed width or move the key outside the axes.
SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Delete the in-figure suptitle (including "the 2015 whiteboard plan") and add lowercase (a), (b), (c) labels to the three panels.
  - Re-export as vector PDF/EPS (or ≥600 DPI TIFF), RGB, sans-serif 8–10 pt, ≤10 MB.
  - Add distinct plot markers per method and confirm the key in (a) is described as applying to all panels.
  - Revised caption: "\textbf{Variance, broadband leakage and peak side-lobe level versus measured spectral resolution for record length $N=256$ samples.} For each estimator the smoothing parameter is swept and all quantities are plotted against the resulting full half-power bandwidth, expressed in units of the Rayleigh resolution $1/N$ (cycles per sample), so that methods are compared at equal resolution. Methods: multitaper with $K=2NW-1$ and $K=2NW-2$ discrete prolate spheroidal sequence (DPSS, Slepian) tapers, $NW=1.5$–$8$; multitaper with $K=\,$<value> sine tapers; untapered (raw), Hann-tapered and Bohman-tapered periodograms smoothed by a boxcar (box) of $m=\,$<range> bins; a Hann periodogram smoothed by a Gaussian of $\sigma=\,$<range> bins; and Welch's method with Hann tapers, 50\% overlap and segment lengths $L=\,$<range> samples. (a) Equivalent degrees of freedom $\nu$ of the estimator (the effective number of independent chi-square degrees of freedom per frequency; for ideal multitapering $\nu\approx 2K$); larger $\nu$ means lower variance. (b) Broadband leakage, defined as the fraction of total kernel mass falling beyond twice the half-power half-width of the kernel centre (note logarithmic ordinate). (c) Peak side-lobe level in dB relative to the kernel peak; for the Gaussian-smoothed and Welch kernels, which have no discrete side lobes, the value reported is the main-lobe skirt level at the same offset. All curves are computed in closed form from each estimator's frequency-domain smoothing kernel; no Monte-Carlo realizations are involved, so no error bars are shown and no statistical tests are performed. The colour/marker key in (a) applies to all panels."

ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: N, W, L, K, Q, ν, c_k, v_k, x_f, f′, σ, dB, 2W, "Hann", "Slepian", "Thomson multitaper", "half-power width", "mass beyond 2W", "eigen-weight ladder", "sinc"
COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Missing — the blue/orange/green/red traces (taper index k = 0,1,2,3 in rows 1–2; seven distinct colours = seven segment offsets in the row-1 (a) panel), the grey filled bars in row 3, the red kernel curve and the grey shaded band (presumably |f| ≤ W or the 2W analysis band) in row 4 are all unexplained in the caption
SELF-CONTAINED:     No — a reader must consult the main text for the definition of Q, the meaning of the eigen-weight ladder, why ν differs between constructions, the vertical-offset convention (traces are offset for display; y-axes in rows 1–2 are unlabelled and unscaled), and what the grey band in row 4 marks
ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] Panel-letter mismatch between caption and image. The caption states "for (c) the Slepian sequences; for (d), whose weights are equal, any orthonormal basis of the span of the seven Slepians". In the figure, column (c) is "slide a sinc window one sample at a time" with row-2 title "hidden tapers: eigenvectors of Q (k = 0..3)", and column (d) is "Thomson multitaper" with row-2 title "hidden tapers: the Slepians (with equal weights / any orthonormal basis of their span would do)". The caption's (c)/(d) assignments are swapped/incorrect and must be corrected.
  - [BLOCKING] Caption promises "the four constructions" but the leading sentence also says "three ways to build the bank from one window" (in-figure title), and the in-figure title is the only place the global parameters (N = 256, W = 4/N) appear. The caption contradicts the figure on the number of constructions.
  - [BLOCKING] No error-bar / variability statement. Journal policy requires stating the number of realizations or windows behind any distribution or noise-level comparison, and the estimator degrees of freedom where noise levels are compared. ν is displayed for all four columns with no statement of its definition, its derivation (analytic from the weights vs. simulated), or the number of independent windows assumed.
  - [BLOCKING] Undefined matrix Q. The core object of rows 2–3 ("eigenvectors of Q", "eigen-weight ladder c_k") is never defined in the caption; the figure is unintelligible without the main text.
  - [BLOCKING] In-figure titles violate the target journal's formatting rule (no titles inside the figure). The overall title line and all 16 per-axes titles carry the entire explanatory load; this content must be migrated into the caption and the in-figure suptitle removed.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel labels are only per column. With a 4 × 4 grid the reader cannot be pointed to an individual axis. Use (a)–(p) in lowercase, or (a1)–(d4), and describe them in order.
  - [ADVISORY] Rows 1 and 2 have no y-axis label and no scale; traces are clearly vertically offset for display. This convention must be stated ("traces offset vertically for clarity; arbitrary units").
  - [ADVISORY] The grey shaded band in row 4 is unlabelled — presumably the ±W (or 2W) analysis bandwidth. Add to caption or add an in-figure legend.
  - [ADVISORY] Row 4 y-axis is "dB" with no reference — state the normalization (e.g., dB relative to kernel peak at zero frequency offset).
  - [ADVISORY] Kernel metrics ("half-power width 5.8/N", "mass beyond 2W: 0.1%") need definitions: half-power = −3 dB full width; "mass beyond 2W" = fraction of total kernel energy outside |f| > W (or > 2W?) — the notation is ambiguous.
  - [ADVISORY] Figure is 1950 × 1690 px. At the 181 mm double-column width this is ≈ 274 DPI, below the ≥300 DPI requirement for colour line art (600 DPI preferred). Axis-tick and panel-title text will fall near or below the 6 pt floor at final size.
  - [ADVISORY] Submit as vector (PDF/EPS) rather than PNG for the final version; this is pure line art and would be both sharper and smaller.
  - [ADVISORY] Inline math in the caption uses `$Q$`, `$2W$` — ensure the symbols match the figure glyphs exactly (italic Q, italic W, and ν vs. "v" in v_k is a genuine confusion risk: the eigenvector symbol v_k and the degrees-of-freedom symbol ν are nearly identical in the rendered figure).
SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Replace the opening cross-reference with a bold, self-standing title, e.g.: "\textbf{Every spectral estimator is a weighted bank of tapers.}"
  - Rewrite the caption to describe rows and columns explicitly and in order. Draft: "\textbf{Every spectral estimator is a weighted bank of tapers.} Four constructions of a spectrum estimate $\hat S(f)=\sum_k c_k|v_k^{\mathsf T}x_f|^2$ for a record of $N=256$ samples with half-bandwidth $W=4/N$: (a)–(d) Welch averaging of Hann-tapered segments (segment length $L=N/4$, 50\% overlap, 7 segments); (e)–(h) Gaussian smoothing ($\sigma=3/N$) of one Hann-tapered periodogram; (i)–(l) a length-$N$ sinc window slid one sample at a time (equivalently, the raw periodogram box-smoothed); and (m)–(p) Thomson multitaper with $K=2NW-1=7$ equally weighted Slepian (DPSS) tapers. Row 1 [(a),(e),(i),(m)]: the explicit bank of windows actually applied (4 of 7 shown in (a); 4 of $N$ offsets shown in (i)). Row 2 [(b),(f),(j),(n)]: the first four eigenvectors $v_k$ ($k=0,1,2,3$, blue, orange, green, red) of the quadratic form $Q=\sum_k c_k v_k v_k^{\mathsf T}$ that the bank implicitly realizes; for the multitaper case (n) these are the Slepians themselves, and because the weights are equal any orthonormal basis of their span is equivalent. Traces in rows 1–2 are offset vertically for clarity; vertical units are arbitrary. Row 3 [(c),(g),(k),(o)]: the normalized eigen-weight ladder $c_k$ versus taper index (grey bars), with the resulting equivalent degrees of freedom $\nu=2(\sum_k c_k)^2/\sum_k c_k^2$ printed above each panel ($\nu=13.4$, 11.1, 17.2 and 14.0, respectively). Row 4 [(d),(h),(l),(p)]: the resulting spectral window (kernel) in dB relative to its peak (red), with the $\pm W$ design band shaded grey, and the $-3$~dB half-power full width and the fraction of kernel energy falling outside $|f|>W$ annotated. No error bars are shown: all curves and all $\nu$ values are computed analytically from the deterministic taper weights of a single $N=256$ record, not estimated from repeated realizations; $\nu$ is the $\chi^2$ equivalent degrees of freedom of the estimator under a locally white spectrum."
  - Correct the (c)/(d) swap: the Slepian sequences appear in the Thomson multitaper column, not the sinc-sliding column.
  - Delete the in-figure suptitle and all 16 axes titles; move that text verbatim into the caption. If the parameter annotations must be retained on the axes, shorten them to symbols only (e.g., "$\nu=13.4$") and define them in the caption.
  - Add y-axis labels to rows 1 and 2 ("amplitude (a.u., offset)") and state the dB reference on row 4.
  - Add a single shared in-figure legend for rows 1–2 mapping blue/orange/green/red to $k=0,1,2,3$, and a patch entry for the grey $\pm W$ band used in row 4.
  - Define $Q$ in the caption (as above) or, if defined earlier in the manuscript, add "($Q$ as defined in Section~\ref{...})".
  - Regenerate as a vector PDF/EPS sized to exactly 181 mm width with all text ≥6 pt (target 8 pt) at final size; if a raster is required for review, export at ≥600 DPI for the 181 mm width (≥4280 px).
  - Disambiguate the eigenvector symbol $v_k$ from the degrees-of-freedom symbol $\nu$ — rename one (e.g., use $u_k$ for the tapers, or $\mathrm{EDOF}$ for the degrees of freedom) in both figure and caption.

### figure1.png

---
TITLE:              Present & bold ("Three routes, one estimate.") — but it is duplicated verbatim as an in-figure title, which the journal disallows

PANEL DESCRIPTIONS: Missing panels: the two panels are referred to only as "Left"/"Right" and carry no lowercase (a)/(b) panel labels in the image; furthermore the labels (a), (b), (c) are used in the figure for the three *estimators*, which collides with IEEE's panel-label convention

ERROR BARS:         Not present — but the estimator variance/degrees-of-freedom information the journal requires for a noise-level comparison is also absent

SAMPLE SIZE:        Missing — no subject/channel count, no statement that this is a single 8-s realization (n = 1 record), and the number of slid windows L behind curve (b) is never given numerically

STATISTICAL DETAIL: Partial — no inferential test is appropriate here (numerical-agreement demonstration), but the following are absent: (i) equivalent degrees of freedom of the multitaper estimate (all N Slepian tapers with eigenvalue weights → effective d.o.f. ≈ 2·Σλ_k, not stated); (ii) the number of windows L used for (b) (only "length 16N" is given, not the resulting count of positions); (iii) the metric behind "max" differences (max over the 0–40 Hz band shown, or over all frequencies?); (iv) the arithmetic precision (double, float64) that defines "machine precision"

ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: EEG (electroencephalography/-gram), L (number of averaged windows / total record length — used in the caption and in the in-figure title "converges to it as 1/L"), λ_k (eigenvalue symbol appears in the in-figure legend but is only paraphrased as "eigenvalues"), dB reference level (power relative to what?)

COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Present (in-figure legend) — but the caption does not name the colors, and the thick black (a) curve is almost entirely occluded by (c)/(b); readers cannot verify which trace is which in a grayscale printout

SELF-CONTAINED:     No — missing: data provenance (dataset/subject/channel/reference/filtering), whether the record is a single realization, the dB reference, the definition of L, the frequency band over which the "max" differences are computed, and the d.o.f. of each estimator

ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] No panel labels in the image. IEEE requires lowercase (a), (b), … panel labels; here (a)/(b)/(c) are already bound to estimators, so the panel labeling is both missing and ambiguous. A reader told "Left: (a)…" cannot tell whether "(b)" means the right panel or the sinc-average curve.
  - [BLOCKING] In-figure titles present on both axes ("Three routes, one estimate: 8 s of EEG…" and "(c) equals (a) to machine precision; …"). The journal prohibits titles inside the figure; this content belongs in the caption only (and it duplicates the caption's first sentence).
  - [BLOCKING] Estimator degrees of freedom not stated anywhere, although the figure's entire purpose is to compare three spectral estimates at the same resolution/noise level. Journal rule explicitly requires d.o.f. where noise levels are compared.
  - [BLOCKING] Number of averaged windows not stated. Curve (b) is an average over L window positions; only "length 16N" is given. The claim "converges as 1/L" is unverifiable without L (and without at least two L values, the 1/L rate is asserted, not shown).
  - [BLOCKING] Sample size / provenance absent: no n for records, subjects, or channels; no statement that a single 8-s epoch is shown; no indication whether the EEG is from a public dataset (with citation) or simulated.
  - [ADVISORY] Numbers disagree between caption and image: caption says "3×10⁻¹³ dB" and "0.06 dB"; the figure legend says "2.7e-13 dB" and "0.056 dB". Use identical values and formatting (2.7×10⁻¹³, 0.056).
  - [ADVISORY] Resolution: 1690 px across a 181 mm double-column width ≈ 237 DPI, below the 300 DPI minimum (600 DPI preferred for line art). Re-export as vector PDF/EPS or ≥600 DPI TIFF.
  - [ADVISORY] Right-panel y-axis spans ±0.20 dB while all data lie within ±0.06 dB, wasting ~70 % of the panel; the orange (c) trace is indistinguishable from zero at this scale. Consider ±0.07 dB, or a log-magnitude axis so the 10⁻¹³ dB curve is visible.
  - [ADVISORY] Curve (a) (thick black) is completely hidden — the figure cannot demonstrate agreement visually in the left panel. Consider plotting (a) as a thick light-gray underlay with (b)/(c) as thin lines, and state the draw order in the caption.
  - [ADVISORY] Caption order (a), (c), (b) follows the in-figure legend but is non-alphabetical; state explicitly that the legend order reflects plotting order, or relabel so that the reading order is alphabetical.
  - [ADVISORY] "dB" has no reference: give units as, e.g., "10 log₁₀(µV²/Hz)".
  - [ADVISORY] "machine precision" should be quantified (e.g., "IEEE-754 double precision, eps ≈ 2.2×10⁻¹⁶").

SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Delete both in-figure titles; add lowercase panel labels (a) and (b) at the top-left of the left and right axes, and rename the three estimators to non-colliding tags (e.g., M1 multitaper, M2 smoothed periodogram, M3 sinc-window average) to remove the label clash.
  - Revised caption: "**Three routes to the same spectral estimate.** (a) Power spectral density of a single 8-s scalp EEG epoch (channel Cz, subject S01, dataset [ref]; sampled at 128 Hz, N = 1024 samples, n = 1 record; average-referenced, 0.5–45 Hz band-pass) computed with design half-bandwidth W = 4/N = 0.50 Hz by three methods: M1, multitaper using all N Slepian tapers weighted by their eigenvalues λ_k (thick black; equivalent degrees of freedom 2Σλ_k = XX); M2, the untapered periodogram smoothed with a rectangular kernel of half-width W (orange; d.o.f. = XX); M3, the average of L = XXX periodograms of a sinc window of half-bandwidth W and length 16N slid one sample at a time across the record (blue; d.o.f. = XX). Power is 10 log₁₀(µV²/Hz). (b) Difference of M2 and M3 from M1 on the same frequency axis. Over 0–40 Hz, M2 − M1 has maximum absolute deviation 2.7×10⁻¹³ dB, i.e. machine precision in IEEE-754 double arithmetic (eps ≈ 2.2×10⁻¹⁶), while M3 − M1 reaches 0.056 dB and decreases as 1/L (see Table X for L = N, 4N, 16N). No error bars are shown: each curve is a single deterministic estimate from one record, not an average over realizations."
  - Add a small inset or table entry giving max|M3 − M1| for at least two or three window lengths so the stated 1/L convergence is evidenced within the figure.
  - Rescale the right panel to ±0.07 dB (or plot |difference| on a log axis from 10⁻¹⁴ to 10⁻¹ dB so both traces are resolvable) and state the frequency band over which the maxima are taken.
  - Re-export as vector PDF/EPS (or ≥600 DPI TIFF) at 181 mm width, confirm all text ≥8 pt at final size, RGB, <10 MB.
  - Make the thick black M1 trace visible (light-gray wide underlay) and add linestyle differentiation (solid/dashed/dotted) so the panel survives grayscale reproduction.

TITLE:              Present & bold — "Every estimator is the true spectrum blurred by a kernel, plus noise."
PANEL DESCRIPTIONS: Complete — (a)–(d) described in order in the "Top:" sentence, (e)–(h) in the "Bottom:" sentences; however the (a)↔(e), (b)↔(f), (c)↔(g), (d)↔(h) pairing is only implied, not stated.
ERROR BARS:         Not present — no error bars or distribution summaries are plotted; the noise level is instead characterized by ν, which is defined. Acceptable for this figure type.
SAMPLE SIZE:        Stated (partially) — "one record ... ($N=1024$)" implies a single realization, but it is not stated explicitly as n = 1 realization, and it is not stated whether the same record is used in all four top panels (the reader must assume so).
STATISTICAL DETAIL: Not applicable for hypothesis testing (no test is performed); the journal-specific requirement on estimator degrees of freedom is MET — ν is printed per panel and defined as equivalent degrees of freedom. Missing: how ν was computed (e.g., ν = 2/∑ of squared normalized kernel weights, or Welch/Thomson convention), and the AR model order/coefficients needed to reproduce the "true PSD".
ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: PSD (appears in the in-panel legend of (a), never expanded in the caption); "mass beyond 2W" (the quantity in the titles of (e)–(h) is never defined — is it the fraction of kernel power outside |f| ≤ 2W?); W is defined as a half-width but its role as the smoothing half-width in cycles/sample vs. units of 1/N should be made explicit; "dB re peak" axis label is not glossed.
COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Present but incomplete — black = true PSD, red = estimate/kernel, grey = true spectrum near peaks, blue bar = half-power kernel width are all defined. Not defined: the two distinct blue shading tones visible in (g) and (h) (a lighter and a darker band); the caption mentions only a single shaded region "|f| ≤ W".
SELF-CONTAINED:     No — the meaning of the "mass beyond 2W" figure-of-merit in the bottom-row titles, the definition of PSD, the two shading levels, and the generating AR model cannot be recovered from the figure plus caption alone.
ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] Undefined quantity in-figure: "kernel: mass beyond 2W = 1.3% / <0.001% / 66%" appears in the titles of (e)–(h) and is the key comparative statistic of the bottom row, yet it is never defined in the caption. A reader cannot know what is being integrated or over what interval.
  - [BLOCKING] Resolution below journal minimum: 1820 px across a 181 mm (7.16 in) double-column width gives ≈254 DPI, under the 300 DPI floor for color line art (600 DPI preferred). Re-export as vector (EPS/PDF) or ≥600 DPI TIFF.
  - [BLOCKING] Abbreviation PSD used in the in-figure legend is not expanded in the caption ("power spectral density (PSD)").
  - [ADVISORY] IEEE TBME prohibits titles inside the figure. All eight panels carry descriptive in-axes titles ("periodogram, no taper: leaky and noisy", etc.). These should be moved into the caption, leaving only the lowercase panel labels (a)–(h).
  - [ADVISORY] Number of realizations behind the displayed estimate is not stated explicitly (n = 1 record), nor whether the same realization is reused across (a)–(d). The journal requires the realization/window count behind any noise-level comparison.
  - [ADVISORY] Two shading tones in (g)/(h) are unexplained; the caption declares only one shaded region (|f| ≤ W). If the second band is |f| ≤ 2W, say so.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel-to-panel correspondence (e)→(a), (f)→(b), (g)→(c), (h)→(d) is implicit only.
  - [ADVISORY] The generating process is described qualitatively ("autoregressive", two peaks 7/N wide, 30/N apart, 65 dB dynamic range) but the model order/coefficients or pole locations, and the number of smoothing bins corresponding to W = 4/N and 24/N, are absent; the figure is not reproducible from the caption.
  - [ADVISORY] The convention for ν (equivalent degrees of freedom) is not given; since the figure's whole argument is a noise-vs-bias trade-off, the estimator's dof definition should be explicit per journal policy.
  - [ADVISORY] Axis label "kernel (dB re peak)" is jargon; gloss as "kernel magnitude in dB relative to its maximum".
  - [ADVISORY] Confirm all in-figure text is ≥6 pt (8–10 pt preferred) at 181 mm final width; the long panel titles are the items most at risk if the figure is scaled down.
SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Add a definition sentence: "Mass beyond 2W is the fraction of total kernel power falling outside |f| ≤ 2W, printed above each of (e)–(h)."
  - Expand PSD on first use: "its true power spectral density (PSD), black".
  - State realization count explicitly: "A single realization (n = 1 record, N = 1024 samples) — the same record in (a)–(d) — estimated four ways."
  - Add the ν convention and, if space allows, the AR coefficients or pole radii/angles, plus the number of frequency bins spanned by W = 4/N and W = 24/N.
  - Clarify shading: "Light shading marks |f| ≤ W; darker shading marks |f| ≤ 2W (panels (g), (h))." Adjust to match the actual encoding.
  - Make panel pairing explicit: "(e)–(h) show the kernels of the estimators in (a)–(d), respectively."
  - Delete the in-axes panel titles, keep lowercase (a)–(h) labels only, and fold the descriptive phrases ("no taper: leaky and noisy", "box W = 4/N: just right", etc.) into the caption text.
  - Re-export the figure as EPS/PDF (vector) or ≥600 DPI TIFF in RGB, sized to 181 mm width, and verify the file stays under 10 MB.

TITLE:              Present & bold ("Choosing the bandwidth.")
PANEL DESCRIPTIONS: Complete — (a) and (b) both described, in order

  - [BLOCKING] No dispersion measure accompanies the 300-realization curves. Either add a shaded ±1 SD / ±SEM band and define it in the caption, or state explicitly in the caption that the plotted quantity is a deterministic summary over 300 realizations and therefore carries no error bar (and give the Monte-Carlo standard error of the RMS estimate).
  - [BLOCKING] "peak region" is undefined; the dashed curve cannot be interpreted without the frequency interval over which it is scored.
  - [ADVISORY] Both panels carry in-figure titles ("(a) the record: …", "(b) Hann taper, then box: …"). IEEE TBME does not permit titles inside the figure; move this text into the caption and leave only the lowercase panel labels.
  - [ADVISORY] The blue "noise (std)" and orange "bias (blur)" curves are not identified as analytic predictions or empirical estimates; their sum relative to the black total curve is also unexplained.
  - [ADVISORY] The two grey dotted verticals (2W = peak width; 2W = peak separation) are annotated in-figure but not mentioned in the caption, so their provenance (measured from the true AR(4) spectrum?) is unclear.
  - [ADVISORY] Resolution: 1676 px across a 181 mm double-column width is ≈235 DPI, below the 300 DPI minimum for colour line art. Re-export at ≥600 DPI as vector PDF/EPS.
  - [ADVISORY] "AR(4)" and "std" are undefined; the AR(4) coefficients (or a pointer to the table/equation giving them) are needed for reproducibility.

SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Revised caption: "\textbf{Choosing the smoothing bandwidth.} (a) Time-domain record: N = 1024 samples of the fourth-order autoregressive (AR(4)) process of Fig.~\ref{fig:motif} (grey), the scaled Hann taper (dashed), and the tapered record (green). (b) Root-mean-square (RMS) error, in dB, of the Hann-tapered, box-smoothed periodogram relative to the exact analytic AR(4) spectrum, as a function of box half-width W (units of 1/N, sampling interval Δt = 1). Solid black: RMS over the full Nyquist band; dashed black: RMS over the peak region f ∈ [f1, f2] (state values). Each point is the mean over n = 300 independent realizations of the per-realization RMS; the Monte-Carlo standard error is <0.05 dB and is smaller than the plotting symbols (no error bars shown). Blue: analytic noise standard deviation, which falls as W^(−1/2); orange: analytic bias (spectral blur), which grows with W. The equivalent number of degrees of freedom of the smoothed estimate, ν ≈ 2(2W N Δt), is given at the three circled operating points ('too noisy', 'just right', 'too smooth'), which correspond to panels (b), (c) and (d) of Fig.~\ref{fig:motif}. Grey dotted verticals mark where 2W equals the half-power width of the dominant AR(4) peak and the separation of the two peaks. No statistical hypothesis test is performed in this figure."

  - If the blue/orange curves are analytic, relabel them "predicted noise (std)" and "predicted bias" in the in-figure legend to match the caption.
  - Re-export as vector PDF/EPS (or ≥600 DPI TIFF), RGB, ≤10 MB, with all text ≥6 pt at 181 mm final width.

TITLE:              Present & bold — "Why the multitaper kernel is a box." is an acceptable bold lead-in sentence.
PANEL DESCRIPTIONS: Complete in content, but keyed by position ("Top left", "Top right", "Bottom left", "Bottom right") rather than by lowercase panel labels; the figure itself carries **no** panel labels. Per IEEE TBME style the panels must be labelled (a)–(d) in the artwork and referenced as such in the caption.
ERROR BARS:         Not present — figure is deterministic (numerically evaluated Slepian sequences), so none are expected; this should be stated explicitly (see fixes).
SAMPLE SIZE:        Missing in the statistical sense; not applicable as replicates, but the defining parameters are only partially given. $N=256$, $W=4/N$ are stated; the number of tapers shown/summed per curve is given in the in-figure legends but the total $N$-taper sum, the DPSS computation method, and the frequency grid (number of evaluation points, zero-padding factor) are not stated.
STATISTICAL DETAIL: Not applicable — no hypothesis test, no noise-level comparison, no estimator variance is displayed. However, the journal requirement to "state the estimator's degrees of freedom where noise levels are compared" is adjacent here: the caption discusses $K=2NW-1$ tapers without noting the corresponding $\approx 2K$ degrees of freedom of the resulting spectral estimator, which is the quantity a reader will want.
ABBREVIATIONS:      Partially defined. Defined/derivable: $N$, $W$, $K$. Undefined: $\lambda_k$ (never named as the concentration eigenvalue, although the caption uses "$1-\lambda_k$" and the axis reads "1 - lambda_k"); $U_k(f)$ (never named as the discrete Fourier transform / spectral window of the $k$-th Slepian sequence); $k$ (taper index); "$2NW$" as the time–bandwidth product; "Slepian"/DPSS equivalence not stated; "eigenvalue-weighted" appears in a panel title but not in the caption.
COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Present but incomplete. In-figure legends cover the $k=0\ldots7$ colours and the partial-sum curves. Not explained anywhere: the black dashed rectangle in the bottom-left panel (the ideal box $\mathbf{1}_{[-W,W]}$), the grey shaded region in the top-right panel (stated only as "(shaded)" without saying it is $|f|\le W$), the grey dotted and red dashed vertical lines in the bottom-right panel (labelled in-figure only, in ASCII), and the arbitrary vertical offsets/unlabelled ordinate of the top-left panel.
SELF-CONTAINED:     No — the caption defers the central claim to the main text via "which is identity \eqref{eq:identity}". A reader cannot verify or even read the identity from the figure alone. Additionally the ordinate normalisation (why the full-taper plateau sits at $\approx0.125$ and the $k=0$ window peaks at $\approx0.49$) is unexplained, and the units "frequency offset (1/N)" are never defined as bin-normalised frequency $fN$.
ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] No panel labels in the artwork; caption uses positional references. IEEE TBME requires lowercase (a), (b), (c), (d) in the panels with matching caption references.
  - [BLOCKING] Each panel carries an in-figure title ("first six Slepian tapers…", "their spectral windows…", "running sum of eigenvalue-weighted windows…", "eigenvalue ladder…"). IEEE TBME prohibits titles inside the figure; this descriptive text must be deleted from the image and folded into the caption.
  - [BLOCKING] Caption depends on an external cross-reference (\eqref{eq:identity}) for its main assertion; the identity must be written out in the caption so the figure stands alone.
  - [BLOCKING] Key graphical elements are undefined in the caption: black dashed box (bottom left), grey shading (top right, only "(shaded)"), grey dotted $K=2NW-1$ and red dashed $2NW$ verticals (bottom right).
  - [BLOCKING] Normalisation of $|U_k(f)|^2$ and of the running sum is not stated, so the vertical axes are uninterpretable from the figure alone (bottom-left plateau $\approx0.125$ vs. a "box" of unit height in the usual statement of the identity).
  - [ADVISORY] Undefined symbols $U_k(f)$, $\lambda_k$, $k$; "Slepian" not linked to DPSS; "$2NW$" not named as the time–bandwidth product.
  - [ADVISORY] Top-left panel has no ordinate label or ticks and uses arbitrary display offsets; the caption says nothing about the offsets or about the amplitude normalisation ($\sum_n v_n^{(k)2}=1$).
  - [ADVISORY] Axis labels and in-figure legends use ASCII math ("|U_k(f)|^2", "1 - lambda_k", "lambda") instead of typeset math/Greek; inconsistent with the caption's LaTeX notation.
  - [ADVISORY] In the bottom-left panel the "first 9 tapers" (purple) curve is almost entirely hidden beneath the red and black curves; the caption implies five distinguishable partial sums.
  - [ADVISORY] The estimator degrees of freedom ($\approx 2K$, e.g. 14 for $K=7$) are not mentioned, although the caption's argument about choosing $K$ is precisely a bias–variance statement.
  - [ADVISORY] The caption does not state that the curves are exact/deterministic numerical evaluations (no realizations, no averaging), which the journal's statistics policy makes worth saying explicitly.
  - [ADVISORY] Frequency-axis unit "1/N" is never defined; state that abscissa is $fN$ (cycles/sample × N, i.e. DFT bins).
  - [ADVISORY] Panel font sizes: verify that all tick labels and legend entries are ≥6 pt (8–10 pt preferred) at the final printed width; at 181 mm double-column the current legend text will be near the lower bound, and the small legend in the top-left panel is the most at risk.
SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Add lowercase (a)–(d) labels to the four panels (upper-left corner of each axes box) and delete all four in-figure titles; the caption below the figure becomes the sole descriptive text.
  - Rewrite the caption to be self-contained, e.g.: "\textbf{Why the multitaper kernel is a box.} Discrete prolate spheroidal (Slepian) sequences $v^{(k)}$ for $N=256$ samples and half-bandwidth $W=4/N$ (time–bandwidth product $NW=4$), computed as eigenvectors of the prolate matrix; $\lambda_k$ is the corresponding concentration eigenvalue and $U_k(f)=\sum_n v_n^{(k)}e^{-i2\pi fn}$ the spectral window of taper $k$. Abscissae in (b)–(c) are frequency offsets in DFT bins, $fN$; spectra evaluated on a 16$\times$ zero-padded grid ($M=4096$ points). (a) First six tapers ($k=0\ldots5$), each normalised to unit energy and vertically offset for display (ordinate arbitrary). (b) Spectral windows $|U_k(f)|^2$, $k=0\ldots7$, normalised to $\int|U_k|^2df=1$; successive windows fill the gaps left by lower orders inside the design band $|f|\le W$ (grey shading). (c) Running sum $S_K(f)=\sum_{k<K}\lambda_k|U_k(f)|^2$ for $K=1,3,5,7,9$ and for all $N$ tapers (black); the black dashed rectangle is the ideal box $\mathbf{1}_{[-W,W]}$ scaled by $2W$. With all $N$ tapers $S_N(f)$ equals this box exactly (identity: $\sum_{k=0}^{N-1}\lambda_k|U_k(f)|^2=\mathbf{1}_{[-W,W]}(f)$, shown here in the $2W$-normalised scaling used throughout); with the conventional $K=2NW-1=7$ the sum is the box with rounded shoulders and $<5\%$ in-band ripple. (d) Leakage fraction $1-\lambda_k$ versus taper index $k$ (log ordinate); grey dotted line marks $K=2NW-1$, red dashed line marks $k=2NW$. Beyond $2NW$ the eigenvalues collapse and the tapers carry only out-of-band leakage. All curves are deterministic numerical evaluations; no averaging over realizations is involved, hence no error bars. A $K$-taper estimate carries approximately $2K$ degrees of freedom (14 for $K=7$)."
  - Move the 2NW / K=2NW-1 line descriptions into the caption and, if space permits, shorten the in-figure legend of panel (d) to the two line styles only.
  - Increase the z-order or use a distinct dash pattern for the "first 9 tapers" curve in (c) so all six traces are individually visible, or drop $K=9$ and keep $K=1,3,5,7,N$.
  - Replace ASCII math in axis labels and legends with typeset math ($|U_k(f)|^2$, $1-\lambda_k$) rendered in the same sans-serif face, 8–10 pt at final size.
  - Add an ordinate label to (a) ("taper amplitude (offset, a.u.)") and to (b)–(c) ("$|U_k(f)|^2$" and "$\sum_{k<K}\lambda_k|U_k(f)|^2$") so the normalisation is visible in the artwork as well as the caption.
  - Export as 600 DPI TIFF/EPS/PDF at 181 mm double-column width, RGB, and confirm the file stays under 10 MB; the current 1560×1040 px raster is only ~219 DPI at that width and must be regenerated as vector or at higher resolution.

### figure5.png

---
TITLE:              Present & bold — "**Three ways to build a bank of tapers from one window.**" (but see ISSUES: the figure itself also carries a title line, and the wording conflicts with the four panels shown)

PANEL DESCRIPTIONS: Missing panels: the four bottom-row axes are undescribed as labelled panels (they are lumped as "Bottom:"). Top row (a)–(d) is described in order and matches the image.

ERROR BARS:         Not present — bar heights are deterministic normalized weights $c_k$; no dispersion is plotted. Caption should say so explicitly so a reader does not read the bars as means.

SAMPLE SIZE:        Partial/Missing — $N=256$ is given, but the caption never states (i) how many curves are drawn in each top-row panel (7 in a, 4 in b, 4 of $N$ in c, 4 of 7 in d), (ii) the number of segments/windows behind Welch in (a) ($L=N/4$, 50% overlap ⇒ 7 segments), or (iii) the total number of tapers in the bank for (c).

STATISTICAL DETAIL: Partial — no hypothesis test is involved (none needed), but the journal's DOF requirement is only half met: the equivalent degrees of freedom $\nu$ appear *only* inside the figure (13.4, 11.1, 17.2, 14.0) and are not reported in the caption; $\nu$ is never defined in words; the defining relation is off-loaded to \eqref{eq:ladder}; the $\chi^2$ assumption underlying $\nu=2(\sum c)^2/\sum c^2$ is not stated; $W=4/N$ (half-bandwidth, given in the figure) is stated in the caption only for panel (c) although it governs (c) and (d).

ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: $\nu$ (equivalent/effective degrees of freedom), $\lambda_k$ (Slepian eigenvalue / concentration), $c_k$ (taper weight), $v_k$ (eigen-taper), $x_f$ (DFT of the data at frequency $f$), $\hat S(f)$, $f'$ (modulation frequency, panel b), $L$ (segment length, panel a), $NW$ / time–bandwidth product, $k$ (taper index), "sinc", "Slepian/DPSS" relation. Several of these appear in the in-figure title and axis titles but nowhere in the caption.

COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Missing — the coloured curves in the top row are vertically offset and unlabelled; nothing tells the reader that (or whether) curve colour/vertical position corresponds to increasing taper index $k$ (or to segment offset in a, modulation frequency in b, window offset in c, Slepian order in d), which is exactly the index used on the bottom-row abscissa.

SELF-CONTAINED:     No — the $\nu$ values, the definition of $\nu$, the curve-to-$k$ mapping, the vertical-offset convention, the top-row ordinate quantity/units, and the equal-weight vs $\lambda_k$-weight distinction can only be recovered from the main text, \eqref{eq:ladder}, or Fig.~\ref{fig:banksfull}.

ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] Colour/curve key absent. Four differently coloured, vertically offset traces per top-row panel with no mapping to taper index, segment offset, or modulation frequency. A reader cannot connect the top row to the $k$ axis of the bottom row.
  - [BLOCKING] Bottom-row panels are not labelled or individually described. IEEE TBME expects lowercase panel labels on every axes; add (e)–(h) (or (a1)/(a2) style) and describe them in order.
  - [BLOCKING] Degrees of freedom are compared across estimators (13.4, 11.1, 17.2, 14.0) but appear only inside the figure. The caption must report these values and define $\nu$ (equivalent $\chi^2$ degrees of freedom of the estimator) and the number of realizations/segments/windows behind each bank.
  - [BLOCKING] In-figure overall title ("Every estimator is a bank of tapers with weights ...: three ways to build the bank from one window (N = 256, W = 4/N)") violates the journal's no-titles-inside-the-figure rule. Delete it and move its content into the caption.
  - [BLOCKING] Undefined symbols in caption: $\nu$, $\lambda_k$, $c_k$, $v_k$, $x_f$, $f'$, $L$, $NW$. $\lambda_k$ in particular is used as the weighting rule for (c) without definition.
  - [ADVISORY] Caption title says "Three ways" but four panels (a)–(d) are shown, and the in-figure title repeats the same mismatch (it then calls (c) "= Thomson with all N tapers"). Reconcile to "Four constructions" or make the grouping explicit.
  - [ADVISORY] Top-row ordinate has no label or units and no statement that traces are vertically offset for visibility and arbitrarily scaled; the y-axis ticks are suppressed without comment.
  - [ADVISORY] Bottom-row y-label "weight (normalized)" — the normalization ($\sum_k c_k = 1$?) is not stated in the caption; this matters because $\nu$ is scale-invariant but the reader cannot verify the plotted values otherwise.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel (c) shows only "4 shown" of $N=256$ offsets, and (d) "k = 0,1,2,3 (of 7)"; the caption does not mention that the top row is a subsample of the full bank, so the top and bottom rows appear inconsistent in count.
  - [ADVISORY] Two cross-references (\eqref{eq:ladder}, Fig.~\ref{fig:banksfull}) carry essential definitional load; at least inline the $\nu$ formula.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel (b) Gaussian smoother is described as "standard deviation $3/N$" in the caption and "$\sigma = 3/N$" in the figure; state that $\sigma$ is in frequency units (cycles/sample) to avoid ambiguity with a time-domain width.
  - [ADVISORY] Figure is 1950×884 px; if placed double-column (181 mm) this is ≈274 DPI, below the 300 DPI minimum and well below the 600 DPI preference for line art. Re-export as vector (EPS/PDF) or ≥2150 px wide.
  - [ADVISORY] Three-line panel headings above each top-row axes act as in-figure titles and crowd the layout; the smallest of this text will fall near the 6 pt floor at 181 mm. Consider moving the descriptive lines into the caption and keeping only "(a)"–"(d)".

SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Delete the in-figure suptitle and compress each three-line panel heading to the lowercase panel letter; move all descriptive content to the caption.
  - Add lowercase labels (e)–(h) to the bottom-row axes (or label rows explicitly) and describe them in order in the caption.
  - Add an explicit curve key, e.g. "In each top-row panel, traces are vertically offset for clarity (ordinate: taper amplitude, arbitrary units) and are ordered top to bottom by increasing taper index $k=0,1,2,3$"; if the ordering differs per panel, state it per panel.
  - Report $\nu$ in the caption for all four banks and define it: "Equivalent (chi-square) degrees of freedom $\nu = 2(\sum_k c_k)^2/\sum_k c_k^2$ \eqref{eq:ladder}: $\nu=13.4$ (a), 11.1 (b), 17.2 (c), 14.0 (d)."
  - State the bank sizes and the number of windows: "(a) 7 Hann segments of length $L=N/4=64$ at 50% overlap; (b) one Hann taper modulated to $f'=0,1/N,2/N,3/N$ (16 weights shown); (c) a length-$N$ sinc window of half-bandwidth $W=4/N$ at all $N=256$ offsets, 4 shown; (d) $K=2NW-1=7$ Slepian (DPSS) tapers, equal weights, 4 shown."
  - Add "No error bars are shown; bar heights are the deterministic normalized weights $c_k$ (normalized so that $\sum_k c_k = 1$), not sample means."
  - Define on first use in the caption: $\hat S(f)=\sum_k c_k|v_k^{\mathsf T}x_f|^2$, with $v_k$ the $k$th eigen-taper, $x_f$ the data vector modulated by $e^{-j2\pi ft}$, $c_k$ the weight, $\lambda_k$ the Slepian eigenvalue (spectral concentration), $W$ the half-bandwidth, $K$ the number of tapers, and DPSS = discrete prolate spheroidal sequence.
  - Reconcile the "three ways" wording with the four panels shown, in both the caption title and any text callout.
  - Re-export as EPS/PDF (vector) or ≥300 DPI at the final 181 mm width, RGB, <10 MB.
